# META
~~~ini
description=Misspelled accept payload names and attributes stay unresolved and payloads do not leak across transitions
type=file
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package AcceptPayloadUnresolved {
    private import ScalarValues::*;

    item def Command {
        attribute enabled : Boolean;
    }

    state def Machine {
        entry; then idle;
        state idle;
        state running;

        transition misspelledPayload
            first idle
            accept command : Command
            if comand.enabled
            then running;

        transition misspelledAttribute
            first running
            accept command : Command
            if command.enabeld
            then idle;

        transition leaked
            first idle
            accept other : Command
            if command.enabled
            then running;
    }
}
~~~
